Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the cell_biology structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 992. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 773 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the cell_biology decomposition. By minimality, the element a^992 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 773-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
